### Four-camera lossless source encoding

`signal_control.py:FourCameraEncoder` now writes all four named BGRA Native sensor streams to separate FFV1 `.mkv` files under the prospective capture profile (1920×1080 overview and three 1280×720 detail views, 20 fps).
